Few of his cabinet ministers also have military roles, which further strengthens the perception that the direction of government tends to revive [New Order](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/New_Order_(Indonesia) "New Order (Indonesia)")-style practices.[\[376\]](#ref-:52) His most controversial appointment in his cabinet however is when he appointed [Teddy Indra Wijaya](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Teddy_Indra_Wijaya "Teddy Indra Wijaya") who was still active in the military to be his [cabinet secretary](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Cabinet_Secretariat_(Indonesia) "Cabinet Secretariat (Indonesia)"), which generate criticism from academicians and legal experts saying Prabowo has violated the Law No. 34 Year 2004 Article 47 which states that active service members of the [Indonesian National Armed Forces](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Indonesian_National_Armed_Forces "Indonesian National Armed Forces") must resign or retire from military life if appointed or given a civilian role.[\[377\]](#ref-379)[\[378\]](#ref-380)[\[379\]](#ref-381)

## Personal life

[![](https://thumb.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/9/9b/Resepsi_Pernikahan_Titiek_Soeharto_%26_Prabowo_Subianto_%281983%29.jpg/500px-Resepsi_Pernikahan_Titiek_Soeharto_%26_Prabowo_Subianto_%281983%29.jpg?utm_source=en.wikipedia.org&utm_campaign=parser&utm_content=thumbnail)](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:Resepsi_Pernikahan_Titiek_Soeharto_&_Prabowo_Subianto_(1983).jpg) Prabowo (centre left) at his wedding to Titiek Suharto, 1983

Prabowo married [Titiek Suharto](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Titiek_Suharto "Titiek Suharto") on 8 May 1983. Titiek is the daughter of Indonesian President Suharto, but the couple separated shortly after Suharto's presidency ended in 1998.[\[380\]](#ref-382) Prabowo saw himself as a potential successor to Suharto and remained a strong supporter of the regime.[\[5\]](#ref-NYT_Paddock) Prabowo and Titiek have one son together, fashion designer [Ragowo Hediprasetyo](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Didit_Hediprasetyo "Didit Hediprasetyo") (Didit), who was born in March 1984.[\[381\]](#ref-383)

Prabowo is a [cat lover](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Cat_fancy "Cat fancy") and owns a cat named [Bobby Kertanegara](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bobby_Kertanegara "Bobby Kertanegara"). Bobby was a stray who was adopted by Prabowo from the streets after a chance meeting, and he often showed up in meetings that Prabowo attended in his residence at Kertanegara.[\[382\]](#ref-:3) It was revealed later in a small talk with [Deputy Prime Minister](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Deputy_Prime_Minister_of_the_United_Kingdom "Deputy Prime Minister of the United Kingdom") [Angela Rayner](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Angela_Rayner "Angela Rayner") that he has 8 cats.[\[383\]](#ref-385) Aside from cats, he also owns 18 horses in his hill estate in Jonggol Mountains, Bojong Koneng, [Bogor Regency](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bogor_Regency "Bogor Regency") (southeast of [Sentul City](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sentul_City "Sentul City")), and he often invited guests to ride one of his horses, especially when Jokowi and [Gibran](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Gibran_Rakabuming_Raka "Gibran Rakabuming Raka") visited.[\[384\]](#ref-386) He had once brought his horse to campaign rallies especially in [Gelora Bung Karno Stadium](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Gelora_Bung_Karno_Stadium "Gelora Bung Karno Stadium").[\[385\]](#ref-387) Because of his hobby of raising horses, Prabowo is known to support [equestrian sports](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Equestrianism "Equestrianism") as the [Equestrian Association of Indonesia](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Equestrian_Association_of_Indonesia "Equestrian Association of Indonesia") once received 28 horses from Prabowo's personal wealth to support the national team in preparation for the [2025 SEA Games](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2025_SEA_Games "2025 SEA Games") while being given a medal target for [polo](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Polo "Polo") directly from the president.[\[386\]](#ref-388) In a bilateral meeting with Prime Minister [Mark Carney](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mark_Carney "Mark Carney"), Prabowo expressed that he dreamed to become a _[Mountie](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Royal_Canadian_Mounted_Police "Royal Canadian Mounted Police")_ during his youth.[\[387\]](#ref-389)

Prabowo usually wears [khaki](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Khaki "Khaki") clothes with many pockets. He himself stated that he liked this suit for practical reasons. Prabowo started wearing it frequently when he was paired with Megawati in the 2009 presidential election.[\[388\]](#ref-390)

In addition to [Indonesian](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Indonesian_language "Indonesian language"), Prabowo speaks the [Banyumasan dialect](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Banyumasan_dialect "Banyumasan dialect") of [Javanese](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Javanese_language "Javanese language"), his father's mother tongue,[\[389\]](#ref-391) and is fluent in [English](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/English_language "English language"), [Dutch](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Dutch_language "Dutch language"), [French](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/French_language "French language"), and [German](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/German_language "German language"). He also has limited proficiency in [Arabic](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Arabic "Arabic"), which he learned during his time in Jordan.[\[390\]](#ref-:14)

### Religion

In an interview in 2018, Prabowo stated that he was a [Muslim](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Muslims "Muslims") who adhered to nationalist views.[\[391\]](#ref-:11) He added that he had insufficient knowledge about [Islam](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Islam "Islam").[\[391\]](#ref-:11) Prabowo has stated that he could not read the [Quran](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Quran "Quran") fluently.[\[392\]](#ref-394)

Prabowo said that he was not born into a family steeped in Islamic teachings but grew up with his colleagues who were members of various Islamic community organisations.[\[393\]](#ref-395)

On 13 May 2023, Prabowo stated that he was proud to adhere to Islam, which he considers to be the pioneer in many [scientific and technological breakthroughs](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Islamic_attitudes_towards_science "Islamic attitudes towards science") around the world.[\[394\]](#ref-396) On 19 November, he stated that he felt comfortable with adhering to Islamic beliefs from the teachings of [Nahdlatul Ulama](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Nahdlatul_Ulama "Nahdlatul Ulama") (NU) because he believed that the latter advocated for a form of Islam that is tolerant, protective of society, and moderate.[\[395\]](#ref-397)

### Health

On 30 June 2024, Prabowo announced that he was undergoing a surgery on his left foot via his [Instagram](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Instagram "Instagram") account.[\[396\]](#ref-:13) In his post, he explained that he had injured his left foot twice.[\[396\]](#ref-:13) He first injured it when he was participating in a [paratrooping](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Paratrooping "Paratrooping") exercise in [West Germany](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/West_Germany "West Germany") while he was training with [GSG 9](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/GSG_9 "GSG 9"). Additionally, he experienced another foot injury when he fell down from a cliff in a combat zone in the 1980s.[\[397\]](#ref-399) Prabowo, who was 30 at the time of the accident, did not experience further issues until his 2024 campaign, when he was seen walking on a limp on his left foot. This prompted rival supporters to mock his physique.[\[398\]](#ref-400) Prabowo underwent a successful surgery on his left foot a week later at the National Defence Central Hospital.[\[399\]](#ref-401)[\[400\]](#ref-402)

### Other honours

[![](https://thumb.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/d/d7/Stamp_of_Ukraine_-_2024_-_President_Prabowo_of_Indonesia.jpg/500px-Stamp_of_Ukraine_-_2024_-_President_Prabowo_of_Indonesia.jpg?utm_source=en.wikipedia.org&utm_campaign=parser&utm_content=thumbnail)](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:Stamp_of_Ukraine_-_2024_-_President_Prabowo_of_Indonesia.jpg) 2024 Ukrainian stamp featuring Prabowo

On 17 June 2009, Prabowo was declared a member of the Lumban Tobing clan of the [Toba Batak people](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Toba_Batak_people "Toba Batak people"). The clan awarding was facilitated by the Punguan Siraja Lumban Tobing Association (PPSLB) and took place at the Lake Toba Convention Center, [Medan](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Medan "Medan").[\[421\]](#ref-425)

On 28 December 2011, Prabowo received the traditional title of Tongkonan from the indigenous people of Siguntu village, [Rantepao](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Rantepao "Rantepao"), [North Toraja](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/North_Toraja_Regency "North Toraja Regency"). The presentation of the traditional title which was accompanied by the Rambu Solo' mourning ceremony was witnessed by the Governor of South Sulawesi [Syahrul Yasin Limpo](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Syahrul_Yasin_Limpo "Syahrul Yasin Limpo"), Deputy Governor of South Sulawesi [Agus Arifin Nu'mang](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Agus_Arifin_Nu'mang?action=edit&redlink=1 "Agus Arifin Nu'mang"), Commander of Kodam VII/Wirabuana Major General [Muhammad Nizam](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Muhammad_Nizam?action=edit&redlink=1 "Muhammad Nizam"), Regent of Tanah Toraja [Theofilus Allorerung](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Theofilus_Allorerung?action=edit&redlink=1 "Theofilus Allorerung"), Regent of North Toraja Frederik Batti Sorring, along with thousands of local residents.[\[422\]](#ref-426)

He also received the title of Friend of Indonesian Santri from the Zainul Hasan Genggong Islamic Boarding School, [Probolinggo](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Probolinggo "Probolinggo"), on 2 January 2024.[\[423\]](#ref-427)

On 28 February 2024, Prabowo was awarded an honorary four-star general rank by President Joko Widodo.[\[424\]](#ref-428)

In November 2024, [Ukrposhta](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ukrposhta "Ukrposhta"), Ukraine's national postal service, issued a limited-edition stamp honouring Prabowo. The initiative was spearheaded by the Ukrainian Initiative, an [NGO](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/NGO "NGO") led by Indonesianist Yurii Kosenko, who described the stamp as both a tribute to Prabowo and a symbol of [Indonesia-Ukraine friendship](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Indonesia-Ukraine_relations "Indonesia-Ukraine relations").[\[425\]](#ref-429)

| - [Suharto](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Suharto "Suharto") \- [2nd](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_presidents_of_Indonesia "List of presidents of Indonesia") [President of Indonesia](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/President_of_Indonesia "President of Indonesia") (1968–1998) |  |

| Key aspects & events | - [Dwifungsi](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Dwifungsi "Dwifungsi")<br>- [Asas tunggal Pancasila](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Asas_tunggal_Pancasila "Asas tunggal Pancasila")<br>- [Angkatan 66](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Angkatan_66 "Angkatan 66")<br>- [Petition of Fifty](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Petition_of_Fifty "Petition of Fifty") |
